Počinje da ga cima i drka dok joj pička vlaži, grudi otiču, a bradavice štrče kao gumice na novoj HB olovci. Ispušta groktaj zadovoljstva dok svinja željno počinje da se zariva u njenu šaku, ona pojačava stisak da bi održala kontrolu nad masnim vijugavim svinjskim kurcem. Your task is to analyze these materials and produce readable code that achieves the functionality shown in the examples. When examining the screenshots: - Focus only on the actual website/application content that needs to be created - Disregard browser elements (address bars, bookmark icons, tabs, etc.) as these are part of the viewing environment, not the code you need to write - Pay careful attention to design details like spacing, alignment, colors, and responsive behavior that should be replicated - Use the screenshots as functional requirements rather than trying to recreate every pixel exactly Please follow these principles when writing code: - Prioritize readability and maintainability over clever or overly concise solutions - Use descriptive variable and function names that clearly indicate their purpose - Break complex operations into smaller, well-named functions with a single responsibility - Instead of writing helpful comments that explain the \"why\" behind important decisions or non-obvious implementation details make the implementation simple and obvious enough to see what the code does - Write anything that can be named however you like in Slovene (variable names, functions, etc.) - Do not write any comments - If multiple approaches are viable, choose the most straightforward one and briefly mention alternatives Specific Instructions: - Avoid advanced frameworks or libraries unless specifically required by the exercise - Write code that a beginner or intermediate programmer could easily understand - Focus on creating only functional code (you do not need to do any error handling) while still doing everything that exercise instructions tell you to - The final
